         format = DATETIME22.3
         informat = DATETIME22.3
         label = 'DtAlocacao',
      DmOperacaoCredito.DtInclusao length = 8
         format = DATETIME22.3
         informat = DATETIME22.3
         label = 'DtInclusao',

         format = DATETIME22.3
         informat = DATETIME22.3
         label = 'DtAlteracao',

         format = 11.
         informat = 11.
         label = 'CdModalidadeCatalogoProduto',

         format = $60.
         informat = $60.

         informat = $10.

      DmOperacaoCredito.CdRatingClienteBacen length = 2
         format = $2.
         informat = $2.
         label = 'CdRatingClienteBacen'
   from
      work.W3VZFTDF as W3VZFTDF,
      PDDDM.DmOperacaoCredito as DmOperacaoCredito
   where
      W3VZFTDF.CdOperacaoLimiteCredito = DmOperacaoCredito.CdOperacaoLimiteCredito
   ;
quit;

      options nosyntaxcheck;

      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      /* Reset syntaxcheck option to previous setting  */
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;

%let etls_recnt = 0;
%macro etls_recordCheck;
   %let etls_recCheckExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia, VIEW)));

   %if (&etls_recCheckExist) %then
   %do;
      %local etls_syntaxcheck;
      %let etls_syntaxcheck = %sysfunc(getoption(syntaxcheck));
      /* Turn off syntaxcheck option to perform following steps  */
      options nosyntaxcheck;

      proc contents data = PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia out = work.etls_contents(keep = nobs) noprint;
      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      /* Reset syntaxcheck option to previous setting  */
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;
%etls_recordCheck;

%macro etls_recordCheck;

      /* Turn off syntaxcheck option to perform following steps  */

      proc contents data = pddstagi.StgOperacaoGarantia out = work.etls_contents(keep = nobs) noprint;
      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      /* Reset syntaxcheck option to previous setting  */
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;

         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;

%macro etls_recordCheck;

      %let etls_syntaxcheck = %sysfunc(getoption(syntaxcheck));
      /* Turn off syntaxcheck option to perform following steps  */
      options nosyntaxcheck;

      proc contents data = PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia out = work.etls_contents(keep = nobs) noprint;
      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      /* Reset syntaxcheck option to previous setting  */
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;

/*---- Define load data macro  ----*/

/* --------------------------------------------------------------
   Load Technique Selection: Update - Matching=ModifyByColumn, New=ModifyByColumn
   Blanks may replace non-blanks: true
   Constraint and index action selections: 'INIT','ASIS','ASIS','ASIS'
   Additional options selections...
      Set unmapped to missing on updates: false

%macro etls_loader;

      modify PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia

      /* if the record does not exist in the master, then add it  */
      if %sysrc(_DSENMR) eq _iorc_ then
         output;
      /* if the record exists in the master, then replace it  */
      else if %sysrc(_SOK) eq _iorc_ then
         replace;
      %if &etls_StopOnIORC ne %then
      %do;
         if _iorc_ in (&etls_StopOnIORC) then
         do;
            etls_msg = iorcmsg();
            put etls_msg;
            %rcSetDS(5)
            stop;
         end;
      %end;
      _iorc_ = 0;
      _error_ = 0;

   run;

   %let etls_StopOnIORC=; /* reset to blank */

%let etls_recCheckExist = 0;
%let etls_recnt = 0;
%macro etls_recordCheck;
   %let etls_recCheckExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia, VIEW)));

   %if (&etls_recCheckExist) %then
   %do;
      %local etls_syntaxcheck;
      %let etls_syntaxcheck = %sysfunc(getoption(syntaxcheck));
      /* Turn off syntaxcheck option to perform following steps  */
      options nosyntaxcheck;

      proc contents data = PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia out = work.etls_contents(keep = nobs) noprint;
      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      /* Reset syntaxcheck option to previous setting  */
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;
%etls_recordCheck;

%macro etls_recordCheck;

      options nosyntaxcheck;

      proc contents data = PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia out = work.etls_contents(keep = nobs) noprint;
      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      /* Reset syntaxcheck option to previous setting  */
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;

%let etls_recnt = 0;
%macro etls_recordCheck;
   %let etls_recCheckExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia, VIEW)));

   %if (&etls_recCheckExist) %then
   %do;
      %local etls_syntaxcheck;
      %let etls_syntaxcheck = %sysfunc(getoption(syntaxcheck));
      /* Turn off syntaxcheck option to perform following steps  */
      options nosyntaxcheck;

      proc contents data = PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia out = work.etls_contents(keep = nobs) noprint;
      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      /* Reset syntaxcheck option to previous setting  */
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;
%etls_recordCheck;

   update PDDDM.DmOperacaoGarantia
      set
         VrTotalGarantia= VrTotalGarantia  + VrMaqEquipamentos
      where
         VrMaqEquipamentos > 0
   ;
quit;

%rcSet(&syscc);

%mend etls_sql_update;

         informat = 11.
         label = 'CdSegmentoMercado',

         format = $50.

         format = 20.6
         informat = 20.6
         label = 'VrAvalPessoaFisica',

         format = DATETIME22.3
         informat = DATETIME22.3
         label = 'DtAlocacao',
      DmOperacaoCredito.DtInclusao length = 8
         format = DATETIME22.3
         informat = DATETIME22.3
         label = 'DtInclusao',

         format = $2.
         informat = $2.
         label = 'CdRatingClienteBacen'
   from
      work.W3VZFTDF as W3VZFTDF,
      PDDDM.DmOperacaoCredito as DmOperacaoCredito
   where
      W3VZFTDF.CdOperacaoLimiteCredito = DmOperacaoCredito.CdOperacaoLimiteCredito
   ;

%macro etls_recordCheck;

      options nosyntaxcheck;

      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      /* Reset syntaxcheck option to previous setting  */
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;
